1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

1 群益投顧 
市 場 流 傳 一 份 疑 似 台 達 電(2308 TT) 發 給 客 戶 的 通 知 指 出，特 定 批 次 電 源 供 應 器 存 在 潛
在 品 質 問 題 。 此 次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件 ， 並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計 ， 且 台 達 電 擁 有
不 同 電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決， 影 響 有 限。 台達電
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 ， 受 惠 於AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC 電 源 大 幅
成長外， CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成
長動能 ， 投 資 評 等 為 Buy ，目標價為 2,050 元。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

電 容 器 更 換 事 件 對 營 運 影 響 有 限：  
市場流傳一份疑似台達 電 發給客戶的通知指出，特定批次電源供應器存在
潛 在 品 質 問 題 。內 容 表 示 根 據 調 查 ， 受 影 響 PSU 所 使 用 的 部 分 電 容 器 ， 在
未 施 加 電 壓、長 時 間 存 放 後，可 能 出 現 漏 電 流 增 加 情 況，特 定 條 件 下 恐 進 一
步 造 成 電 容 器 電 壓 不 平 衡 及 過 熱，重 新 上 電 時 可 能 導 致 電 容 器 故 障。此 次 涉
及台達 電 新一代 VR 平台 18.3 kW PSU ， 相 關 問 題 於 試 產 階 段 發 現 ， 台 達電
隨 後 通 知 客 戶 並 進 行 電 容 器 更 換 。 
台達 電 表 示 不 評 論 市 場 傳 聞；若 相 關 情 況 確 認 屬 供 應 商 零 組 件 問 題，公 司 將
依 實 際 狀 況 更 換 供 應 商 ， 並 配 合 客 戶 需 求 進 行 後 續 處 理 。 
此次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件，並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計，且 台 達電 擁 有 不 同
電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ，
營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評 等 為Buy ， 目 標 價 為 2, 050 元。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
環境保護：聯發科技做為全球半導體產業技術領導者，在整體價值鏈中位於
最前端的晶片設計，後端的原物料採購、代工製造、封裝與測試等環節皆交
由供應商執行，形成專業分工與互相合作的夥伴關係。根據風險調查與碳足
跡盤查發現，主要對社會與環境產生衝擊影響的關鍵在於後端製造作業，故
如何與供應商共同合作，以降低社會與環境相關的潛在風險，為聯發科技發
展綠色營運的首要任務。  
社會責任：運用數位科技解決社會問題不是企業的專利，聯發科技相信每個
人都有潛力利用數位科技創造無限可能，聯發科發起數位社會創新競賽 「智
在家鄉」，鼓勵社會大眾為家鄉問題提供解方。  
公司治理：聯發科技董事會下設有審計委員會，強化公司治理獨立性，並設
立薪資報酬委員會，以健全公司薪酬制度為目標，持續強化企業永續發展。
除前述法定功能性委員會外，本公司亦設立法定以外之併購策略委員會，視
實際需求針對集團具體併購案件及整體併購策略評估分析。  
產品組合：  
2Q26 產品組合：手機晶片業務 41% 、智慧裝置平台業務 53% 、 PMIC 6% 。  
手機業務全年展望保守：  
2Q26 手機晶片業務營收 QoQ-14%\YoY-20% ，營收占比 41% ，衰退原因是
手機的 BOM 表的成本上漲，聯發科預計 2026 年全球手機出貨量 YoY 約 -
15% 。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。 3Q26 手機業務展望季持平或季減中個位數百分
比，旗艦級 SoC 的量產可以抵消大部分其他手機業務的衰退。  
客戶重視旗艦級產品與價格敏感的入門級產品 ， 聯發科 3Q26 將推出 2nm 製
程的 SoC ，為下一代 AI 模型提供動力，聯發科與全球廠商合作，持續擴大
2027 年旗艦級手機的市占率。  
智慧邊緣平台業務展望正向：  
2Q26 智慧邊緣平台業務營收 QoQ+19\YoY+26% ，營收占比 53%。成長動能
包含在運算、車用的市佔率的提升，以及 TV SoC 的成長。多項連接、車用
項目走向量產，聯發科預計此業務將季增中高個位數營收。  
2Q26 PMIC 營收占比約 6%：  
2Q26 PMIC 業務營收 QoQ+11%\YoY+6% ，營收占比 6%。成長動能包含資
料中心的市占率提升，聯發科預計此業務將季持平。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

3 群益投顧 
AI ASIC 業務展望更樂觀：  
聯發科第一個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 4Q26 開始量產，預計 2026 全年資料中
心將貢獻超過 20 億美元，並且在 2027 年倍數成長，聯發科預計 AI ASIC 在
2027 年的市場規模將達到 800 億美元，聯發科目標市佔率從 10~15% ，上修
至 15~20% 。第二個 AI 加速器 ASIC 將於 2028 年初量產，聯發科有信心在
2028 年的市占率將提升。  
聯發科認為每代的 ASIC 的毛利率相當，並不會更高， ASIC 的毛利率低於
目前公司平均，但新世代的 ASIC 對營收的貢獻貴更大。  
其他補充：  
聯發科 448G SerDes 的研發順利，採用 CPO 的解決方案。 448G SerDes 之
後，聯發科會在台積電 (2330 TT) 的 COUPE 平台上，繼續發展 CPO 的解決
方案。聯發科還有提供 3.5D 的平台，該平台提供 IP、封裝與設計流程，適
用於大型資料中心的設計。  
供應鏈成本上漲已經是產業現象，聯發科將對價格進行調整，以確保成本上
漲可以反映在產品定價中。聯發科目前還在投入新技術，絕對費用金額還會
再增加，但是由於營收規模上升，費用率會下降。

  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

•MediaTek becomes a much more credible AI ASIC contender . MediaTek 
already expects its first major AI accelerator ASIC to enter production in 
4Q26, has guided to >$2bn of data-center revenue in 2026, estimates a 
roughly US$80bn 2027 addressable/serviceable market, and has raised its 
target share to 15–20%.   The NVIDIA relationship therefore potentially 
expands MediaTek’s opportunity beyond its existing hyperscaler programs: 
NVLink Fusion can become a customer-acquisition platform for future 
second customer's ASIC wins as NVLink Fusion changes the competitive 
proposition: instead of MediaTek selling customers only an independently 
designed ASIC, it can potentially offer a custom XPU already engineered to 
coexist with NVIDIA GPUs, CPUs, networking and rack-scale NVLink 
infrastructure. According to NVIDIA, the cooperation will be deepening 
including : NVIDIA NVLink Fusion chiplet, connecting XPUs to the NVIDIA 
NVLink scale-up fabric using NVIDIA photonics or electrical 
interconnects;  NVIDIA NVLink-C2C, providing high-bandwidth, energy-
efficient connectivity between XPUs, NVIDIA Rosa CPUs and other 
compatible processors. NVIDIA NVHBM, integrating customized memory 
capabilities to increase bandwidth and energy efficiency while dedicating 
more silicon area to compute.
•MediaTek is evolving from a smartphone SoC company into an AI 
compute-platform . MediaTek and NVIDIA announced the GB10 platform 
last year, where MediaTek contributed Arm CPU/SoC and power-efficiency 
expertise to the Grace Blackwell-based DGX Spark platform.   That 
relationship has already expanded into RTX Spark for Windows AI PCs, and 
today’s announcement explicitly says the two companies intend to

collaborate across multiple generations of both RTX Spark and DGX Spark products.   MediaTek therefore gains three
separate AI growth vectors: (1) hyperscaler custom AI ASICs, (2) NVIDIA-based AI PCs/local AI systems, and (3)
automotive/physical AI. Strategically, this is important because MediaTek can reuse the same core capabilities—
leading-edge CPU/SoC design, SerDes/interconnect, advanced packaging, connectivity and power optimization—

across all three markets. The result should be a progressively higher mix of large, advanced-node chips with much
greater dollar content than traditional smartphone SoCs, reducing MediaTek’s dependence on handset cycles.
•We view today’s announcement as structurally positive for MediaTek. The US$3.5bn investment validates the
partnership financially, it potentially moves MediaTek from being a supplier that happens to have a few large AI ASIC
wins toward becoming one of the principal custom-silicon design platforms inside the broader NVIDIA AI-factory

ecosystem. That could materially increase both the number and longevity of MediaTek’s AI ASIC opportunities in the
longer term.

### Page 3

Mediatek (2454.TW)
31 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 2

Unimicron (3037.TW)
03 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Unimicron
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect
Unimicron's ability to generate stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a market risk
premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target
price is equivalent to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus PE multiple of the past five

years. However, as the market focuses on 2027 upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI
business; 2) significant increase in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure from
depreciation due to higher capex investments.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

For the month of September 2026, we estimate AirTAC’s monthly revenue will increase 
to Rmb827mn (+6% MoM, +17% YoY)/NT$3,930mn (+6% MoM, +27% YoY) 
We expect September revenue will go up by 6% MoM to Rmb827mn/NT$3,930mn, 
mainly driven by the improvement in daily pneumatic revenue (Rmb30.9mn in Sep vs. 
Rmb 29.1mn in Aug) despite the same number of working days. The improvement in 
daily pneumatic revenue is driven by the strong growth from key customers, particularly 
in electronics (we also expect peers to see strong demand in electronics, see here), 
followed by batteries and machine tools, and we expect demand to improve following 
the inventory pull-in after the Europe summer holiday.

Investment view: Strong YoY momentum in electronics could drive overall growth. 
Maintain positive view on AirTAC 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC, as we continue to see key end-segments 
performance tracking ahead of the company’s 2026 full year guidance of 20%+ YoY

### Page 3

growth (see here), with electronics/battery/machine tools delivering 20%/64%/31% 
YTD YoY growth while total revenue in Rmb is tracking at 25% YTD YoY growth. The 
strong YTD performance is in line with our expectation that AirTAC is a key beneficiary 
of the factory automation/industrial automation localization trend in mainland China, as 
well as benefiting from the prolonged upcycle supported by government stimulus 
policies. However, we maintain caution on the potential of exaggerated orders (including 
advance or duplicate orders), and will continue to monitor the underlying end-demand 
closely. 
Furthermore, we believe the company is well-positioned to continue to gain market 
share in the China pneumatic market, particular given its pricing strategy, (1) its 
pneumatic products pricing remain competitively priced at 10%+ below international 
peers, and (2) the company has maintained stable pricing in China (China accounts for 
~95% of total revenue in 2026E per GSe) amid the inflationary cost environment, while 
the company will continue to enhance internal operational efficiency to mitigate the 
rising cost risk (see here). As a result, we continue to see backlog accumulating and 
expect the company to maintain a high UTR to digest these orders. 
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,925. 
Investment Thesis - AirTAC 
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share 
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality 
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast 
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner 
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC 
continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing 
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and 
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure 
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring 
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to 
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring. 
Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its 
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the 
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to 
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E 
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC. 
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,925 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on 
our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle). 
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected 
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in 
mainland China.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
On the latest earnings call, Broadcom's CEO noted that the company is building
substrate capacity in its Singapore factory with a partner. We believe he was
referring to AST, the JV Broadcom has been developing with Toppan (7911.T, Not
Covered) since March 2024 (news). This is Toppan's first overseas substrate plant,
with phase 1 of the two-phase fab targeted to start production by EoY 2026. We see

Broadcom securing FC-BGA substrate supply rather than bringing production in-
house, and therefore expect limited impact on the current supply positions of
Unimicron and NYPCB.
On BT substrate pricing, the price hikes since 2H25 have been driven by raw material
cost inflation, including T glass, gold and copper. We have expected T glass supply
constraints to peak in 2026 and gradually ease from 2027 as more suppliers are
qualified. Thus, it would not surprise us if raw-material-driven price hikes plateau

going forward. That said, our OW thesis on Unimicron and NYPCB centers on ABF
substrate tightness, so we see limited impact from BT price fluctuations on our
ratings for both stocks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
NYPCB's share price was down 12% in the past two trading days (vs. TAIEX up
0.8%).
Investors have asked us if NYPCB would face share losses if Broadcom (AVGO,
covered by Joe Moore) were to produce substrates on its own.
Separately, we have been receiving questions from investors about the pricing
outlook for BT substrate in 4Q.

We see limited impact on our OW thesis for Unimicron and NYPCB, both of which
produce BT substrates and are Broadcom's incumbent suppliers.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 2

ramp-up of D2C satellites from 2H27, with PCB area consumption to further increase by
40-50%, and (3) the ramp-up of AI satellites from 2H27, with PCB dollar content value
that is similar to LEO satellites, but at a significantly larger annual deployment volume of
several tens of thousands of units (vs. 3-4K LEO satellites launched by the key LEO
customer in 2025).
(6) The company mentioned that it is seeing some impact from material shortages, but

did mention the supply conditions for low-end CCL are improving, as supply has been
primarily constrained by E-glass. However, management mentioned there are no supply
issues for high-end CCL, while supply is well-supported by key CCL suppliers. In terms of
rising material costs, the company said it can pass through cost increase across nearly all
products, except for SLP products for the key smartphone customer.
(7) The company said it is seeing multiple growth opportunities for its data center PCB

business, including (1) strong demand for high-end PCB for AI satellites from its key LEO
customers (as mentioned in bullet point 4), (2) PCB for AI servers from the key
smartphone customer, which is expected to ramp-up from 4Q26, (3) additional
contributions from China customers with PCB demand across switches and AI servers,
and (4) an ongoing project with a small-scale ASIC client.
PCB/CCL sector read-across
We retain our positive view on the optical transceivers’ mSAP demand outlook, while our

supply chain checks recently suggested mSAP as a key bottleneck for the 1.6T optical
transceivers supply chain now, whose product pricing has gone up by 30%+ YTD with
good profitability (our checks and Compeq’s comments suggest the GM for 1.6T optical
transceivers mSAP are generally 40-45%+). We believe ZDT has the best opportunity to
gain more market share in this high-growth and profitable market (we expect TAM for
1.6T+ optical transceivers mSAP to go up from US$0.8bn in 2026 to US$2.9bn in 2028,

implying 94% CAGR), considering it has the most aggressive mSAP capacity expansion
plan (we expect ZDT to expand mSAP capacity by 5x in the next two years) and the
company’s best-in-class BT/mSAP production technology, which could become the
company’s key revenue driver (we expect mSAP to account for ~8%/10%+ of ZDT
revenue in 2027/28 from ~2% in 2026) and margin driver (mSAP GM should be 40-45%+
vs. ZDT 1H26 GM at only ~22+%).
We also expect CCL suppliers, such as EMC and TUC, to benefit from the strong optical

transceiver demand, and we expect both companies to gain more share with more CCL
capacity to be ready in 2027/28 (see here and here).
Overall, we retain our positive view on ZDT’s business outlook not only on mSAP
products, but also seeing a significant margin expansion opportunity from the high-end
AI PCB and ABF substrate products, where we also see a strong demand outlook (see our
AI PCB industry report here and our ABF substrate industry report here).

We maintain our Buy ratings on ZDT, EMC and TUC with unchanged 12m TPs of
NT$925/NT$10,200/NT$3,010.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology 
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared 
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF 
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most 
from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) due to 
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also 
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive 
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see 
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to 
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.  
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$925 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology, 
including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for 
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x 
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in-line with industry downcycle 
average valuation).  
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower 
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate 
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market. 
Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
7 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Taiwan PCB/CCLFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

### Page 3

Earnings revision: We factor in VPEC’s Jul-Aug revenues and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 12% / 18% / 30%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. 2026-28E revenues 
are revised up by 7% / 14% / 25%, mainly on higher contribution from optoelectronics 
business, driven by the strong end demand of AI data center optical networking, better 
InP substrate supply, and VPEC’s customers’ expansion and product expansion from CW 
lasers to EML, along with specification upgrade. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised up on 
rising contribution from optoelectronics business, which carries a higher margin than 
other product lines. We revise up 2027-28E opex ratios, mainly on higher R&D expense 
to collaborate with key customers on next generation optical networking products.

### Page 8

VPEC focuses on III-V compound semiconductor EPI wafer for microelectronics and 
optoelectronics devices based on InP and GaA substrate, and expands to EPI wafer for 
SiPh transceivers CW laser for data center applications. We expect strong momentum in 
VPEC growth driven by the SiPh ramp up in 2026E, microelectronics clients’ increasing 
demand, and its accumulated technology advantages on EPI wafer. We note that VPEC is 
currently trading lower than our TP implied 69x 2027E forward P/E, which is attractive, 
in our view.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - VPEC 
Valuation: We adopt a discounted P/E method and apply a 22.2x target P/E on VPEC’s 
2029E EPS, discounting back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6% 
and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12m TP of NT$793. Our target P/E is 
based on 0.4x PEG&M ratio derived from the average level of peers in the photonics 
supply chain. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh business ramp up; weaker-than-expected 
microelectronics demand from smartphones clients; IDM offers EPI wafer in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Hiwin Technologies Corp.  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +1% MoM/+42%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Hiwin's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$2,708mn (+1% MoM/+42% YoY).
• QTD revenue was 67% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$7,996mn (+6% QoQ/
+33% YoY), and 68% of consensus, at NT$7,884mn (+5% QoQ/+32% YoY).
Our view:
• We think 3Q26 is tracking slightly ahead of expectations.

• Looking into 2H26, we maintain the view that Hiwin should continue to see
revenue growth amid the industry recovery and margin expansions driven by
higher utilization/price hikes.
• We stay constructive on the stock, with current valuation at 29x 2027e P/E
vs. a peak cycle level of 35-40x.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.

n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
TFT-LCD Panel Price Outlook
for Sep-26: TVs, Monitors and
NBs Flat MoM
Panel pricing should stat flat for major applications in the
coming months. We believe that the glass opportunities in
advanced packaging are already in the price for panel makers,
though the timing for mass production and intensifying
competition could be potential risk factors.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Derrick Yang

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
S. Korea Technology
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
Exhibit                                                                             1                        :                    TFT-LCD TV Panel Price Trends

Jan-18 Jul-18 Jan-19 Jul-19 Jan-20 Jul-20 Jan-21 Jul-21 Jan-22 Jul-22 Jan-23 Jul-23 Jan-24 Jul-24 Jan-25 Jul-25 Jan-26 Jul-26

75" 4K 65" 4K 55" FHD 50" FHD 43" FHD 32" HD
Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay flat MoM in September: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and
75" TV panel prices are all expected to stay flat for the month of September. This is
consistent with our expectations. On the demand side, although overall demand is
lackluster, there is still some seasonal restocking demand from brand customers to
prepare for  year-end promotion campaigns. On the supply side, panel makers remain

disciplined on output control and plan some annual maintenance to adjust
utilization during the coming October holidays in China. On a quarterly average
basis, we expect TV panel prices to decline by low-single-digit % QoQ in 3Q26.
IT panel prices to stay flat MoM in  September: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to stay flat MoM during the month. Both applications are  seeing
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about

increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 3Q26.
Risk/reward less attractive: We think the current valuation for Taiwanese panel
names AUO (2409.TW; 1.5x P/B) and Innolux (3481.TW; 1.7x P/B) have already baked
in some expectations of glass in advanced packaging starting to make revenue
contribution in 2028. Nonetheless, some supply chain players think this might take

longer, and there seem to be many suppliers from different supply chains vying for
this opportunity as well, which could be a potential risk to monitor. In China, we
prefer BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL (000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale
advantage and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Seasonal restocking along with disciplined output control should help stabilize
TV panel prices in the coming months.
IT panel prices are expected to stay roughly flat in coming quarters as well.
Potential opportunities for glass in advanced packaging seem  already  priced in,
with the risk in commercialization timing and competition worth monitoring.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trending Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM and August was down 1% MoM, in line with our expectation that
TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That said, we do not expect a
significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide downside support.

Below-Seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization To Be at ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance

following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.
Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, while we expect a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing

structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to
profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Fair After Recent Correction
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display, and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.7x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end-demand: If end-demand for major applications turns out to be
better than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Update
E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales +20% MoM/+3%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$3,446mn (+20% MoM/+3% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 63% of our 3Q26 revenue estimate of NT$9,985mn (-2%
QoQ/-4% YoY) and 62% of consensus at NT$10,213mn (flat QoQ/-2% YoY).
Our view:
• The solid rebound in the Aug monthly sales should alleviate the potential

risk of missing full-year guidance again (now at 10-15% YoY for 2026), in our
view, as  3Q26 revenue is tracking in line with our estimate for  a modest
decline QoQ.
• While the worst seems to be behind us and valuation is at a trough level of
12x 2027 P/E, we see no strong catalysts in the near term, despite our
positive long-term view on  e-paper demand across various applications.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus

EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

積極轉向 HDD、半導體、軍工等高毛利率業務 
 給予買進，目標價 80 元：投顧認為正達具備半導體應用、無人機與
AI 等題材。且 HDD 業務 2027 年第四季月產能達 500 萬片，未來年
產值可達約 60 億元，營運可望出現爆發成長，給予買進，目標價 80
元。 
 預期 3Q26 毛利率優於前期，加以認列廠房處分利益，EPS 2.07 元：
展望 26H2，正達表示營運高峰有望在 3Q26，且閒置的銅鑼三廠出售
預期在 3Q26 完成交割。整體而言，投顧估計正達 3Q26 營收 6.28 億
元，+13.2%QoQ，+6.1%YoY，毛利率回升，加以認列銅鑼三廠處分
利益，EPS 2.07 元。 
 HDD 需求夯，正達積極拚擴產：正達近期積極切入 AI、無人機、半導
體等高毛利率領域。正達以超低平坦度拋光、奈米級剪薄、3D 曲面鍍
膜等技術切入 HDD 供應鏈，目前已建置月產能 10 萬片，預計 2026
年底擴至月產能 100 萬片，2027 年第四季達月產能 500 萬片，2028
年過後若滿載年產值約達 60 億元， 以淨利率15%為假設 ， 可貢獻EPS 
3~4 元。至於軍工與無人機領域與美國國防系統整合商、北約系統商
簽訂多項框架協議，合作航太軍工級玻璃適形天線與軍用無人機，若
順利 2027 年第 2 季可望開始出貨。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號         Page 2 of 5 
個股報告

正達 產品為 玻 璃 的 精 密 加
工，主 要 業 務 為 光電 、車 用 、
建築 等應用  
 正達(3149) 成立於 1996 年 6 月，2011 年 11 月掛牌上市 ，為台灣最大的光電玻
璃加工服務供應商，擁有玻璃切割、強化、薄化、鍍膜等完整加工服務。總部位
於台灣苗栗，生產基地位於台灣苗栗、中國觀瀾及成都、越南。主要產品包括：
保護玻璃、光學鍍膜玻璃、ITO 鍍膜玻璃，產品應用於蘋果電腦及平板、汽車、
建築。主要客戶為蘋果、藍思、群創、GIS-KY。正達與美商康寧簽署車載玻璃
戰略合作備忘錄，在汽車座艙與顯示應用上，結合上游玻璃材料與下游加工能
力，有利守住車用高階市場，並同步受惠國際品牌導入新設計 。

Exhibit 3: 產品組合  
2023 2024 2025 2026F
智能建築 12% 10% 10% 10%
智能汽車 32% 28% 10% 17%
智能光電 55% 62% 80% 73%

Sources : Company、MasterLink

預期 3Q26 毛利率優於前期，加
以認列廠房處分利益，EPS 2.07
元 
 正達 2Q26 營收 5.54 億元，-6.1%QoQ，-2.1%YoY，營收下滑主要是因蘋果議
價導致，毛利率從前其-4.1%升至-3.2%，營業費用為 0.89 億元，營業損失約為
1.07 億元，稅後淨損 1.08 億元，EPS -0.48 元。展望 26H2，正達表示營運高
峰有望在 3Q26，且閒置的銅鑼三廠出售預期在 3Q26 完成交割。整體而言，投
顧估計正達 3Q26 營收 6.28 億元，+13.2%QoQ，+6.1%YoY，毛利率回升，加
以認列銅鑼三廠處分利益，EPS 2.07 元。

正達表示 2026 年成長動能主要來自智能汽車 產品，智能建築及智能光電將下
滑；預期今年底智能汽車營收占比將從目前的 14%升至 20%。整體而言，正達
預期 2026 年營收可維持小幅成長局面，且毛利率受到產品組合優化，預計將持
續上升，投顧預期正達 2026 年營收 23.83 億元，年增 4.4%。

HDD 需 求 夯，正 達 積 極 拚 擴
產  
 正達近期積極切入 AI、無人機、半導體等高毛利率領域。隨 AI 伺服器帶動 3.5 
吋 HDD 需求，HAMR 對高溫玻璃基板的需求，未來大容量 HDD 以玻璃碟盤
為主。正達以超低平坦 度拋光、奈米級剪薄、 3D 曲面鍍膜等技術切入， 目前
已建置月產能 10 萬片，預計 2026 年底擴至月產能 100 萬片，2027 年第四季
達月產能 500 萬片，2028 年過後若滿載年產值約達 60 億元，以淨利率 15%為
假設，可貢獻 EPS 3~4 元。

至於軍工與無人機領域 與美國國防系統整合商、北約系統商簽訂多項框架協
議，合作航太軍工級玻璃適形天線與軍用無人機，產品已在無人機、無人船實

測，若順利 2027 年第 2 季可望開始出貨。另外正達於 2026 年初成立正達戰術

年 9 月開始少量出貨，公司目標是學習曲線提早，目前主要應用於高階車款如

鈞興-KY(4571) 
買進, TP 190 (潛在報酬 22.98%)  
買進 
Price(09/03)：154.5 
Target Price：190

鈞興 26Q2 受惠於電動工具與機器人齒輪銷售強勁，帶動營收維
持年增 ；高毛利產品佔比提升與越南廠規模經濟發酵 ，亦成功緩解
了匯兌損失的干擾，單季 EPS 達 1.75 元。 
展望後市， 智能傳動為公司核心成長動能 ， 目前工業機器人接單暢
旺 。 在產能佈局上 ， 越南廠稼動率維持滿載 ， 二期廠房預計於 2026 
年底投產，將有助於紓解產線瓶頸並迎合美系 客戶供應鏈轉移趨
勢。此外，針對人形機器人產品開發的 「漸開線少齒差減速器」 已
送樣客戶，若認證順利，可望於 2027 年挹注營運。   
綜上所述 ， 研究部預估鈞興 26/27 年營收為 32.74 億 / 34.75 億
元，EPS 分別為 9.76 元與 11.10 元。評價方面，雖短期需求略
受宏觀局勢影響 ， 但考量後市客戶新品上市將帶動營收成長 ， 且產
品組合優化能持續挹注獲利表現 ， 故給予歷史本益比區間中上緣之 
18 倍 PER 評價，目標價 190 元，投資建議為買進。  
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
鈞興主要銷售客製化的全鋼齒輪、粉末治金齒輪、齒輪箱、精
密五金件及諧波減速器等予客戶 ， 生產基地位於廣東珠海廠及
越南廠，產品廣泛用於電動工具、園林工具、工業縫紉機、及
工業機器人等領域，

⚫ 產能優化抵銷匯率之獲利稀釋，26Q2 單季 EPS 為 1.75 元 
26Q2 單季 EPS 為 1.75 元，整體營收達 7.27 億元，QoQ 
-7.19%，YoY 8.15% 。儘管受傳統淡季影響致使營收呈現季
減，但受惠於電動工具與智能傳動產品銷售堅挺，其中電動工
具齒輪與機器人齒輪為主要貢獻，帶動營收仍維持年增表現。 
本季毛利率達 34.24%，季減 0.26 個百分點，年增 3.92 個
百分點。毛利率年 增主要來自(1)產品組合持續優化，高毛利
的機器人齒輪佔比提升，同時低毛利的齒輪箱佔比下降 ；(2)
越南廠規模經濟效益持續擴張 ， 有效抵銷了人民幣升值帶來的
負面影響(3)單季認列存貨跌價回升利益 126 萬元 。 整體獲利
方面，越南廠的生產優化成功緩解了匯率波動帶來的獲利稀
釋，惟本季業外認列了 1,581 萬人民幣對美元之匯兌損失。

⚫ 電動工具與智能傳動客戶需求延續，估 2026 EPS 為 9.76 年 
展望 26H2，預期電動工具受惠於部分客戶新品陸續量產出
貨，營收將有望延續年成長；園林工具則因終端拉貨動能尚未
明顯復甦，恐面臨持平或衰退局勢。智能傳動為公司核心成長
動能，目前工業機器人齒輪接單金額已達2.6 億元，預估全年
貢獻約為 2.3 億元，年成長約三成。總上以上，預期 2026 全
年營收可望維持個位數成長。  
產能利用率方面，越南廠因訂單暢旺而持續滿載，稼動率維持
100%，珠海廠機器人設備相關產線稼動率達 90%至 95%以
上，手工具產線則維持約 8 成水準，隨稼動率與產能持續優
化。預估全年毛利率可為持 33%以上水準。 
總上所述，隨電動工具與智能傳動展望正向，研究部預估鈞興
預估 26/27 年營收為 32.74 億 / 34.75 億，YoY 6.81% / 
6.15%；年毛利率為 33.63% / 33.43%，年增 0.86 個百分點 
/ 年減 0.20 個百分點； EPS 分別為 9.76 元 / 11.10 元。

⚫ 持續佈局越南廠與機器人關節零組件 
為因應客戶供應鏈轉移趨勢，越南廠營收佔比已成漲至3 成。
越南二期廠房預計於 9 月底完工並進行消防與環保驗收 ， 目標
於 2026 年底投產，有助於紓解目前產線滿載之瓶頸，進一步
就近滿足客戶美系客戶之訂單需求。 人形機器人產品方面，
公司產品” 漸開線少齒差減速器“已送樣客戶，若客戶認證順
利，有望於於 2027 年加入貢獻。

⚫ 結論與建議: 
鈞興 26Q2 受惠於電動工具與機器人齒輪銷售強勁 ， 帶動營收維持

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關
條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
1 
4770 上品(ASC)

報告日期： 2026/09/07           研究員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一.漲價轉嫁與高毛利訂單出貨，2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.11 元 
上品主要產品為氟素樹脂「鐵氟龍」半成品材料生產、內襯設備
加工及工程服務，半導體應用佔比近 9 成。2Q26 營收 13.0 億元，
QoQ 23.0%，YoY 0%；營業毛利 4.7 億元，QoQ 25.6%，YoY -5.2%，
毛利率 35.6%較去年同期下降 1.74 個百分點，主係部分出貨來自
去年景氣相對疲弱時接下的訂單，議價空間受限、毛利率偏低，
隨著下半年漲價轉嫁與高毛利訂單陸續出貨，預計毛利率將回
升；營業利益 3.0 億元，QoQ 42.3%，YoY -15.1%，營益率 23.0%；
稅後純益 2.5 億元，QoQ 23.4%，YoY 13.0%，純益率 19.1%，稅後
EPS 3.11 元。

7 月營收 5.3 億元，MoM 12.0%，YoY 47.7%；1-7 月累計營收 28.9
億元，YoY 9.6%。

二. 半導體建廠帶動的化學品供應 ， 訂單能見度至 28 年 
產品組合方面，內襯裝置佔 68.3%；其他材料與內襯管路分別佔
16.7% 與 9.6%；工程收入佔 5.4%。內襯裝置部分，隨著先進製程
對 particle(顆粒污染)、metal contamination(金屬污染)的要求越來越
嚴格，上品的材料升級能力可以配合 2 奈米以下製程需求，從原
料端到終端裝置完整供應。

上品中國市場營收佔比約 44.8% ，中國市場毛利率 33% ，略低於台
灣的 36%，對整體獲利結構的影響較高。台灣市場佔比約 49.3%，
受半導體產業發展持續帶動，持續有大量專案進行中；美國市場
主要客戶二廠槽車專案，從每月 80 台提升到 140 台，目前洽談訂
單已有 400 餘輛，其中 100 台已下訂並準備運往美國，預估今年
ISO tank 出貨量將較去年同期成長 80%至 90%，需求強勁。

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 稅後 EPS 12.5 / 14.5 元 
預估 2026 年上品逐步自 2025 年底逐月回升，2026 年第二季受集
中出貨帶動，7 月營收已突破 5 億元，預計下半年表現優於上半
年，全年營收預估 51.0 億元，YoY 8.0% ；營業毛利 18.2 億元，YoY 
16.9%，毛利率 35.7%；營業利益 11.8 億元，稅後純益 10.0 億元，
稅後 EPS 12.5 元。

考量台灣及美國半導體廠建廠帶動長期需求，預估 2027 年全年營

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 275 元 
綜合考量 (1)台灣、美國半導體建廠需求強勁 (2)中國同業競爭激
烈使毛利率受到壓抑 (3)全球半導體市場維持穩定成長步調 (4)
上品技術領先，於台灣市場競爭對手少具寡佔地位，並觀察上品
歷史本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 15.6 倍評價合理 ， 故投資建議為 「買
進」，目標價 275 元（19 x 2027 EPS）。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
上品綜合工業股份有限公司（簡稱：上品；代號：4770）成立於
1981 年，總部位於台灣彰化，為全球少數具備從氟素樹脂原料加
工、半成品製造到內襯設備整合能力的一條龍供應商。公司核心
產品涵蓋氟素內襯設備、內襯管件及應用材料，廣泛應用於半導
體及電子級化學品產業的高純化學品儲存、輸送與製程系統，並
服務面板、石化等高腐蝕性化學環境應用領域。上品透過垂直整
合掌握關鍵原料與製程，能提供完整的客製化解決方案，確保產
品在耐腐蝕性、潔淨度及可靠性上的領先水準。公司於台灣彰濱
設有生產基地與研發中心，在中國浙江嘉興及美國亞利桑那均設
有百分之百持股子公司，結合當地業務與工程服務團隊，支援全
球客戶的建廠與設備需求。

截至 2026 年，半導體與電子化學品產業為上品營收主力，合計貢
獻超過八成，客戶涵蓋晶圓製造與先進化學品大廠。隨全球先進
製程擴產與電子級化學品本地化政策推進，公司積極參與台灣、
美國與中國多個新建廠案，並在高階氟素內襯產品市場形成與少
數日系廠商的寡佔格局。

二. 營運概況及展望 
產業概況 
氟素材料與設備產業屬於半導體與電子級化學品供應鏈中不可或
缺的基礎環節，其產品廣泛應用於高純化學品的儲存、輸送、製
程反應與廢液回收等系統，涵蓋化學儲槽、管線、內襯設備及運
輸槽車等。由於先進製程對化學品純度、製程穩定性與安全性的
要求日益嚴苛，氟素材料憑藉其優異的耐腐蝕性、化學惰性與低
金屬離子釋出特性，已成為電子級化學品系統的標準配置，對維
持半導體製程良率具有關鍵影響。近年來，隨著 3nm 、2nm 等先進
節點製程的推進，以及高純化學品用量的增加，氟素內襯與高潔
淨管路系統的市場需求持續成長，並高度仰賴具備客製化工程能
力與垂直整合生產的供應商。

圖表二：上品各產品功能

（資料來源：上品 2025 年年報）

全球市場方面，半導體及電子化學品的產能擴張帶動氟素設備需
求同步提升。根據 SEMI 預估，全球晶圓廠設備投資在 2025 年將
達 1,100 億美元，2026 年進一步成長至 1,300 億美元，其中化學品
供應與儲運系統為建廠基礎設施的重要組成。中國市場自 2025 年

4 
起出現多個大型先進製程與記憶體廠新建案，華東與華北地區為
主要集中區域；美國市場則因本地供應鏈尚未成熟，短中期內對
進口高階氟素設備與 ISO Tank 的需求強勁；台灣持續受惠於先進
製程與封裝擴產計畫，化學品系統升級需求穩定。整體而言，該
產業具備技術門檻高、認證周期長、替代性低等特性，市場呈現
由少數具備高純材料加工與系統整合能力的國際廠商寡佔的格
局，且隨各地建廠高峰啟動，對領先供應商而言，成長機會正持
續擴大。

競爭優勢 
上品在全球氟素材料與內襯設備市場的競爭優勢，核心來自其完
整的垂直整合能力與跨區域佈局。公司能從氟素樹脂原料加工、
半成品製造，到內襯設備組裝一次到位，並具備客製化設計與現
場工程整合的能力，能因應不同化學品特性與製程需求，提供高
純度、耐腐蝕且符合嚴格製程規範的系統解決方案。與少數日系
同業相比，上品在台灣半導體供應鏈的在地化程度與反應速度具
優勢，並透過美國與中國的子公司直接切入當地市場，縮短專案
溝通與交付時程。由於高階氟素設備屬於認證周期長、替代性低
的利基市場，進入門檻高，使現有供應商的市場地位相對穩固，
上品憑藉多年的專案經驗與穩定品質，已在半導體與電子化學品
領域建立寡佔地位。

5 
在競爭結構上，中國市場近年新增韓系與本土廠商參與，使價格
競爭壓力加劇，尤其在中階產品與部分專案上，上品需透過價格
策略、模組化產品及在地製造來維持市佔與毛利。美國市場雖競
爭者較少，但工程公司承包大包專案的模式，使供應商議價空間
受限，必須強化與終端客戶及系統整合商的關係。台灣則因先進
製程擴產持續，對高階內襯與 ISO Tank 等設備需求穩定，為上品
的核心利基市場。整體而言，上品的競爭策略在於強化三大區域
的市場滲透率、持續提升在地工程支援能力，並鎖定高附加價值
產品線，藉此在全球建廠潮與化學品供應鏈升級浪潮中鞏固並擴
大領先地位。

未來展望 
上品將持續聚焦於高階氟素內襯設備與高純化學品儲運系統的技
術深化，並以半導體與電子化學品產業為成長核心。公司將延續
中國市場大型專案的出貨動能，並加速推進北美區 ISO Tank 等高
附加價值產品線的導入，藉由產品標準化與模組化設計，縮短交
付週期、提升客戶轉換意願。面對中國中階產品市場價格競爭激
烈的挑戰，上品將強化嘉興基地的在地製造與採購能力，以降低
成本並縮短專案導入時間，同時透過技術升級與高潔淨等級的產
品差異化，維持在高階市場的毛利水準與市場地位。

長期而言，上品將善用全球建廠潮與化學品供應鏈升級的契機，
持續鞏固在台灣半導體先進製程市場的優勢，並深化與系統整合
商及終端客戶的協作，將業務版圖從設備供應擴展至整體工程解
決方案提供者。此外，公司將逐步布局氟素材料的上游原料與製
程優化，以提升關鍵材料自主性與供應穩定性，降低國際局勢及
匯率波動帶來的營運風險。

二. 預估 2025 / 2026 稅後 EPS 12.5 / 14.5 元

年，全年營收預估 51.0 億元，YoY 8.0% ；營業毛利 18.2 億元，YoY 
16.9%，毛利率 35.7%；營業利益 11.8 億元，稅後純益 10.0 億元，

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 4 日

1 群益投顧 
達興材 (5234 TT) 配 合 友 達(2409 TT) 集 團 參 加 Semicon Taiwan 2026 ， 展 出搭載 系統級
Micro LED CPO 的 光 通 訊 解 決 方 案，群 益 認 為，由 於 資 料 中 心 衍 生 商 機 龐 大，相 關 光
通 訊 訂 單 將 為 達 興 材 長 線 潛 在 營 收 動 能 ， 惟 實 際 業 績 貢 獻 程 度 、 時 程 則 尚 待 觀 察 。現
階 段 達 興 材 營 收 動 能 主 要 來 自 半 導 體 材 料，目 前 已 量 產12 款 產 品，預 計 將 再 有 3~4 款
新產品於 2H26~1H27 量 產 ，2H26 、 2027 年 營 收 展 望 佳 ， 不 過 ， 考 量 電 視 、 螢 幕 、筆
記型電腦等 IT 產 品 景 氣 受 到 記 憶 體 漲 價 影 響 ，2H26 恐 旺 季 不 旺 ， 不 利 於 顯 示 器 材 料
(營收比重超過 70%) 的營收表現，故群益維持對達興材 Neutral 的投資建議。

研 究 員 ： 陳 長 榮   
cj.chen@capital.com.tw

配 合 友 達 集 團 於 Semicon Taiwan 展 出 光 通 訊 解 決 方 案：  
達 興 材 配 合 友 達 集 團 參 加 Semicon Taiwan 2026 ， 展 出 搭 載 系 統 級Micro LED 
CPO 的 光 通 訊 解 決 方 案。 由 於友 達 擁 有 在 面 板 產 業 累 積 30 年的 玻 璃 製 程 相
關 量 產 經 驗 ， 藉 由 光 學 耦 合 、 異 質 整 合 、 精 密 製 造 等3 大 核 心 技 術 ， 以 及 集
團成員的 Micro LED 技 術 ， 積 極 進 軍 光 通 訊 解 決 方 案 ， 並 聚 焦 資 料 中 心10
公 尺 以 內 短 距 離 高 速 傳 輸 需 求 。 
具 體 而 言 ， 此 一 解 決 方 案 乃 由 友 達 進 行 巨 量 移 轉 、 重 佈 線 層 封裝
(Redistribution Layer Packaging ，RDL Packaging) 、 光 學 耦 合 、 系 統 架 構 ，並
整合富采 (3714 TT) 的 Micro LED 光 源 、 鼎 元(2426 TT) 的 Micro PD 接 收 元
件，再於 重 佈 線 層 封 裝 中 運 用達 興 材 的 感 光 性 介 電 絕 緣 材 料。群 益 認 為，由
於 資 料 中 心 衍 生 商 機 龐 大，相 關 光 通 訊 訂 單 將 為 達 興 材 長 線 潛 在 營 收 動 能，
惟 實 際 業 績 貢 獻 程 度 、 時 程 則 尚 待 觀 察 。 
2Q26 稅 後 淨 利 2.29 億 元 ， QoQ+7.24% ：  
達興材 2Q26 營收 12.82 億 元 ， QoQ+6.69% ， 動 能 主 要 來 自 半 導 體 材 料 ， 相
關營收 2.91 億 元，QoQ+26.04% ，營 收 比 重 提 高 至 22.68%(1Q26 為 19.20%) ，
惟 毛 利 率 受 匯 率 影 響 而 未 隨 產 品 組 合 轉 佳 而 提 高 ， 加 上 業 外 表 現 不 及
1Q26(1Q 26 匯兌利益 0.14 億元， 2Q26 匯 兌 損 失 0.01 億元 ) ， 稅 後 淨 利 2.29
億元， QoQ+7.24% 。  
2H26 、 2027 年 營 收 動 能 主 要 仍 將 來 自 半 導 體 材 料：  
展望 2H26 ，營 收 動 能 主 要 仍 將 來 自 半 導 體 材 料，目 前達興材 已 量 產 12 款產
品， 另有 14 款 產 品 送 樣 認 證 中 ，終 端 應 用 涵 蓋 先 進 封 裝 、 先 進 製 程 、 成 熟
製 程 等 領 域 ，並 以 先 進 封 裝 表 現 最 可 期 ， 目 前 已 有2 款 CoWoS 相 關 產 品 量
產 ， 且 有 4 款 新 產 品 送 樣 認 證 中 ， 且 均 有 機 會 將 應 用 領 域 再 擴 及 CoPoS 、
FOPLP 等 領 域。整 體 而 言，達 興 材 預 估 3~4 款 新 產 品 將於 2H26~1H27 量 產，
2H26 、2027 年 半 導 體 產 品 營 收展 望 均 佳，營 收 比 重 有 機 會 提 高 至 25% 以上 。 
此外， 達 興 材 2026 年 資 本 支 出 約 4 億 元 ， 主 要 用 以 因 應 半 導 體產品 開 發 、
量產所需 ， 2027 年更 斥資 22 億元 於 高 雄 橋 頭 興 建 新 廠 ， 預 計 該 廠於 1Q27
動 工 、2028 年 完 工 ， 最 快 2029 年 開 始 貢 獻 營 收 ， 將 為 半 導 體 產 品 後 續 出 貨
成 長 的 後 盾 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

林秋香

兆聯實業 (6944 TT)

高科技產業擴廠帶動公司營運續揚

投資評等維持買進，目標價 976 元，潛在漲幅 37% 
1. 毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
2. 在手訂單續創新高 
3. 因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長

毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
兆聯實業 2Q26 營收為 50.8 億元，YoY+36.1%，略低於預期。其中工程類營收為
39.3 億元(YoY+35.0%)，占比 77%，主要係因在手工程持續去化；服務類營收為
11.5 億元(YoY+40.3%)， 成長轉趨強勁主要係因2H25 開始有一個新的製程使用新
的耗材所致，占比 23%。受惠於 1)美國專案工程執行效率佳，完工進度較預期時間
短、2)新承做專案因業主急於擴廠，對施工進度的考量甚於成本，故公司的接單毛
利率較高，與 3)去年接單時台幣較為強勢，今年台幣趨貶，故 2Q26 工程毛利率上
揚至 28%( vs. 1Q26 的 26.5%)，服務類毛利率則達 30.1% (vs. 1Q26 的 29.9%)，
帶動整體毛利率達 28.4%，優於預期。因此，稅後淨利達 8.7 億元，YoY+70.8%，
亦高於研究部預期。若以除權後股本計算，EPS 為 8.75 元。

在手訂單續創新高 
受惠於 AI 需求強勁，半導體、記憶體、PCB、ABF 產業建廠積極度高，兆聯實業 6
月底在手訂單餘額達 277 億元，分別為國內 73%、美國 25%、新加坡 7%、中國
<5%。公司並於 7 月發布重訊公告接獲美國 50 億元以上訂單，若含此訂單，美國
占比將提升至 40%。 由於在手訂單續創新高 ， 研究部正向看待未來工程類營收成長 ，
並預估 3Q26 營收為 59.6 億元，YoY+24.5%。基於 3Q26 工程類毛利率可望持平
於 2Q26 之假設，預估稅後淨利為 10.4 億元，YoY+51.9%，EPS 為 10.4 元。

因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長 
兆聯實業近期公告將發行兩檔無擔保轉換公司債，合計金額為新台幣 25 億元，主
要用於支應美國營運資金需求 ， 預估此對股權稀釋比例約3%。 因應客戶建廠需求 ，
為取得資本財與服務財優勢，公司於台灣中南部有購置土地或建廠計畫，資本支出
約 10 億元；海外方面，公司將投入 19.5 億元建置美國新廠(第一期)，此廠可望在
2028 年完工。雖然業主隨著發包案量提升而有培養其他供應商的計畫，然因兆聯
實業已與業主合作多年，建廠配合默契佳，與國際競爭者相較亦 有成本費用優勢，
故公司競爭性仍佳。除了優化工程進度以提升毛利率外，公司目前已切入記憶體、
先進封裝、載板的純水系統，因純水系統的毛利率較高，未來若能切入半導體製造
廠的純水系統，則毛利率將再提升，另外，美國新廠完成後，公司服務類營收將加
速成長，毛利率亦將隨之提升。

富邦不同之處 
富邦 2026/2027 EPS(F) 為 38.54/48.79 元，Bloomberg 預估為 37.74/41.11 元。

評價與風險 
兆聯實業聚焦在高科技廠水資源工程，因半導體大客戶先進製程擴展不停歇，將帶
動業績再創新高。評價風險為半導體產業擴張放緩，以及原料與人工成本飆漲。

富邦的股票評等標準 
評等  定義 
買進 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬超過15%  
中立  預估未來6個月內有絕對報酬介於15%與負15%之間 
賣出 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬高於負15% 
未評等 由於富邦目前與該公司有特定交易或沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等  
評估中 目前正在研議個股的投資評等，將於3到6個月內提供投資評等

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

公司研究報告

1 
7689大鵬科(Climax Co.)

報告日期： 2026/09/03 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 大鵬科為安防無線通訊整體解決方案之專業廠商，以
自主研發之 F1 系列無線通訊技術平台為核心，深耕安防
與長照無線通訊利基市場多年，產品 組合具技術差異化
優勢，營收規模穩中求變化  
大鵬科技為無線安防與長照警報設備系統製造商，總部位於台北內
湖，生產基地為台灣台北內湖及汐止。大鵬科技營運主要專注於無
線通訊安防、長照警報系統之研發、製造與銷售。以 ODM 模式，主
要客戶為安防服務提供商，供應產品為安防警報系統、智慧家庭安
全設備、長者遠距智能照護居家設備，深耕歐洲、北美、中南美洲
與亞洲市場，大鵬科技營收比重安防警報系統佔 75%、長照警報系
統佔 2 5 %，大鵬科技銷售區域比重，歐洲佔 6 1 . 8 4 %、南美佔
19.76%、北美佔 14.24%、其他區域佔 4.16%。展望 2H26，儘管 2H26
當前經營層面，面臨電子產品記憶體價格上漲、封測晶圓代工吃
緊、PCB 板(印刷電路板)與 PCB 原材料缺料價漲等，各方面零組件
價格居高不下，宏遠預估公司仍可守住一定程度的產品毛利率，皆
源自於大鵬科技優越之成本管控與產品組合優化能力。

二. 宏遠預估 2026 年安防警報產品營收雙位數成長，毛利
率穩健增長 
隨未來智能家居的普及，以及人們安全意識的提升，家庭安防市場
的需求持續增長。此外，全球老齡化趨勢也推動了對長者遠距智能
照護居家設備的需求上升。大鵬憑藉安全、高效、省電、可靠的無
線通訊技術，及高度客製化的靈活性以及經驗豐富的研發團隊，能
快速回應全球不同地區客戶的需求，在市場中建立強大的競爭優
勢。大鵬科技旗下產品，挾帶自主研發窄頻無線通訊技術，藉此延
伸出 F1/SF1/SFV 無線通訊技術架構，於此多協定通訊技術的自主研
發可以在短期間內滿足客戶需求，具備完整之硬體與軟韌體完整之
解決方案。例如：大鵬科技之安防與長照產品，影像傳輸速度與時
間優於同業，大鵬科技安防產品電池壽命為同業之一倍以上，可供
傳輸距離也為同業產品之三倍以上，大鵬科技旗下產品主訴傳輸技
術(低功耗、長距離大於 2 公里以上傳輸距離、高穿透性、高抗干擾
等技術)均為大鵬科技領先同業之競爭優勢。大鵬科技自主研發之窄
頻無線通訊技術，為大鵬科技提供更多元之軟韌體、硬體整合平台
與市場發展空間。日前，大鵬科技已於台北市內湖購置新土地與廠

三. 2026 年起邊緣 AI 新品加入，產能同步擴充營運增長 
宏遠投顧認為，大鵬科技在技術創新投入不遺餘力，2009 年至 2024
之間，2009 年起大鵬科技即加入 ZigBee、Z-Wave 等國際聯盟， 進
軍智慧家庭市場；2012 年推出首款安防警報 SaaS 平台，其後陸續成
功開發自有專屬的 F1、SF1、SFV 無線通訊技術，自行研發之通訊
技術與規格構築大鵬科技技術護城河。大鵬科技最大的技術壁壘在
於其自主研發之「F1 系列無線通訊技術平台(F1/SF1/SFV)」 ，與國際
大廠相較之下，因為大鵬科技提供較為省電、傳輸距離較遠以及傳
輸效率較高，客戶傾向採用自有的私有通訊協定(F1、SF1、SFV 無
線通訊技術)，而不愛用傳輸容易受到干擾的通用 Wi-Fi 或 Bluetooth
傳輸協定，轉而向大鵬科技下訂單。隨著邊緣 AI 落地，智慧安防概
念提升，樓宇自動化商機即將來臨，企業園區、商辦大樓、社區、
工地與公共場域對安全管理需求不斷提高，科技系統保全正從單點
設備監控，逐步升級為結合 AI、機器人、無人機與雲端平台的智慧
化服務。以大鵬科技產品為例，主打無線傳輸之安全監控產品，透
過即時影像分析與權限辨識，協助場域進行異常偵測、人員辨識與
安全管理，經由大鵬科技自行開發之演算法(Algorithm)直接內建於
終端晶片，引入深度學習與邊緣運算技術，智慧安防將引領安防產
品從過去的「事後舉證(被動式錄影)」演進為「事前預警、即時處置
(主動防禦架構)」，並且，大鵬科技之無線傳輸安全監控產品，可為
客戶實現更低功耗、省電節能之功效，相較於同業提供有線安防產
品，大鵬科技無線產品將具備市場競爭力。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 230 元 
大鵬科技公司競爭優勢為，主要專注於無線通訊安防、長照警報系
統之研發、製造與銷售。以 ODM 模式，供應產品為安防警報系統、
智慧家庭安全設備、長者遠距智能照護居家設備，深耕歐洲、北
美、中南美洲與亞洲市場，深耕安防領域多年，且足以提供值得信
賴之各項產品，對於公司長期營運挹注穩健成長動能。展望 2026
年，預估大鵬科技 2026 年營收為 54.98 億元，+23.25%YoY，毛利率
為 30.67%，稅後淨利為 6.95 億元，稅後 EPS+11.88 元。展望 2027
年，預估大鵬科技 2027 年營收為 63.66 億元，+15.79%YoY，毛利率
30.77%，稅後淨利 8.50 億元，稅後 EPS+14.53 元(以 5.85 億元股本為
基礎計)。市場期待，智慧安全監控與居家保全廠商受惠於家用終端
產品線與邊緣 AI 之滲透率逐步提升，帶動營運步入成長軌道，此
外，由於歐洲 EN 54 火災偵測與警報系統相關標準，隨相關規範更
新及各國當地法規陸續跟進開放，火災偵測與警報系統之市場，開

二.26 年營運概況及 26 年展望 
1.大鵬科技產品以智慧家居與安防照護為主軸 
大鵬科技專注於無線通訊安防與遠距照護設備，透過自主 RF 通訊技
術(proprietary RFcommuni cation technology)及雲端平台整合，為全球
保全與照護業者(ProfessionalSecurity/Telecar e Service Providers) 提供
智慧安全與遠距照護解決方案( S m a r t  S e c u r i t y  a n d  T e lecare 
Solutions)。大鵬科技以 B2B 及 B2B2C 合作模式為主，穩健進軍全球
各大市場經濟體，協助客戶建構從終端設備到雲端平台的整合解決
方案。

2.未來 2~3 年為新品佈局放量，相關產能擴充積極 
根據 Grand View Research 研調機構報告指出，2024 年全球智慧家庭
安控市場規模為 339.4 億美元，預計至 2030 年成長至 820.7 億美元市
場規模，CAGR+15.2%，其中北美、歐洲為 2 大市場。此外，隨居家
安全意識提升，物聯網技術不斷進步，根據 Berg Insight 報告指出，
2024 年底北美約有 3,720 萬、歐洲 1,960 萬用戶家庭或小型企業場
域，需要安裝智慧監控裝置。根據各國政府推動邊緣運算裝置(Edge 
AI)、智慧化城鎮，以及相關使用者系統升級，同時帶動物聯網市場
規模，從 2024 年 3,696 億美元，成長至 2033 年 11,700 億美元，
CAGR 達 13.73%，規模成長不斷加速當中，即使全球安控市場與物
聯網產品市場規模商機龐大，大鵬科技指出以 2026 年市占率仍位居
低個位數(低於 1%)之下，足以顯示大鵬科技未來營運規模成長可
期。
